9084:  function solve(i,mask){

9086:   pool.forEach((e,j)=>{const bit=1n<<BigInt(j);if(mask&bit||!hjRecapEligible(e,plan[i].slotId))return;const tail=solve(i+1,mask|bit),candidate={total:score(j)+tail.total,kept:tail.kept+(j===original[i]?1:0),picks:[j,...tail.picks]};if(!best||candidate.total>best.total+.00001||Math.abs(candidate.total-best.total)<.00001&&candidate.kept>best.kept)best=candidate});
9087:   if(!best){const tail=solve(i+1,mask);best={...tail,picks:[-1,...tail.picks]}}memo.set(key,best);return best;
9088:  }

9228: async function hjRecapLoadExtra(chosen){
9229:  const {week}=chosen;if(HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.jobs.has(week))return HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.jobs.get(week);
9230:  const old=HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.weeks.get(week);if(old&&Date.now()-old.checked<300000)return old;
9231:  const extra={...(old||{}),games:new Map(old?.games||[]),checked:Date.now()};HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.weeks.set(week,extra);
9232:  const job=(async()=>{
9233:   const tasks=[async()=>{extra.pool=await hjDataPool(Number(NFL_SEASON),Math.max(week,hjCurrentWeek()))},async()=>{extra.schedule=await hjDataSchedule(Number(NFL_SEASON),week)},async()=>{const p=await hjDataRequest(`recap-transactions:${NFL_SEASON}:${week}`,()=>hjDataJson(`https://lm-api-reads.fantasy.espn.com/apis/v3/games/ffl/seasons/${NFL_SEASON}/segments/0/leagues/${ESPN_FANTASY_LEAGUE_ID}?view=mTransactions2&scoringPeriodId=${week}`),300000);if(Array.isArray(p.transactions))extra.transactions=p.transactions}];
9234:   await Promise.allSettled(tasks.map(f=>f()));if(HJ_HQ_STATE.activeTab==='recap')hjRefreshRecap();
9235:   const ids=[...new Set(chosen.scores.filter(r=>r.lineupComplete).flatMap(r=>r.starters.map(e=>hjWeeklyStat(e,week)?.externalId)).filter(Boolean))];
9236:   // Two concurrent requests keep background enrichment from flooding live feeds.
9237:   let next=0;async function worker(){while(next<ids.length){const id=ids[next++];try{const game=await hjDataRequest(`recap-nfl:${NFL_SEASON}:${id}`,()=>hjDataJson(`https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/football/nfl/summary?event=${id}`),300000);if(game.header?.competitions?.[0]?.status?.type?.completed)extra.games.set(String(id),game)}catch(_){}}}
9238:   await Promise.all([worker(),worker()]);extra.checked=Date.now();hjRefreshRecap();return extra;
9239:  })().finally(()=>HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.jobs.delete(week));HJ_RECAP_EXTRA.jobs.set(week,job);return job;
9240: }

12298: function hjLineupPlan(data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){

12304: function hjEntryEligible(entry,slotId){return (HJ_OPT_SLOT_ELIGIBLE[Number(slotId)]||[]).includes(hjPlayerPosition(entry))}
12305: function hjActualSelection(entries,plan){
12306:   const starters=entries.filter(entry=>![20,21].includes(Number(entry?.lineupSlotId))),used=new Set();
12307:   return plan.map(slot=>{let entry=starters.find(item=>!used.has(item)&&Number(item?.lineupSlotId)===Number(slot.slotId));if(!entry)entry=starters.find(item=>!used.has(item)&&hjEntryEligible(item,slot.slotId));if(entry)used.add(entry);return entry||null});
12308: }
12309: function hjOptimalSelection(entries,plan,week,mode){
12310:   const pool=entries.filter(entry=>Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)!==21&&HJ_POSITION_ORDER[hjPlayerPosition(entry)]!=null),order=plan.map((slot,index)=>({slot,index,elig:(HJ_OPT_SLOT_ELIGIBLE[Number(slot.slotId)]||[]).length})).sort((a,b)=>a.elig-b.elig||a.index-b.index),memo=new Map();
12311:   function solve(i,mask){
12312:     if(i>=order.length)return {score:0,picks:[]};const key=`${i}|${mask}`;if(memo.has(key))return memo.get(key);const item=order[i],choices=[];
12313:     pool.forEach((entry,pIndex)=>{if(pIndex<30&&!(mask&(1<<pIndex))&&hjEntryEligible(entry,item.slot.slotId))choices.push({entry,pIndex,score:hjProjectionScore(entry,week,mode)})});
12314:     choices.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);let best={score:-1e9,picks:[]};
12315:     for(const choice of choices){const next=solve(i+1,mask|(1<<choice.pIndex)),score=choice.score+next.score;if(score>best.score)best={score,picks:[{index:item.index,entry:choice.entry},...next.picks]}}
12316:     if(!choices.length){const next=solve(i+1,mask);best={score:next.score,picks:[{index:item.index,entry:null},...next.picks]}}
12317:     memo.set(key,best);return best;
12318:   }
12319:   const solved=solve(0,0),selected=Array(plan.length).fill(null);solved.picks.forEach(pick=>selected[pick.index]=pick.entry);return selected;
12320: }

12355: function hjLineupDisplayName(name,position=''){

12358:   /* Team defenses display as the team name only in matchup rows. */
12359:   if(String(position||'').toUpperCase()==='D/ST'||/\bD\/ST\b/i.test(raw))return raw.replace(/\s+D\/ST.*$/i,'').trim()||raw;

12363:   const first=parts.shift();

12365:   /* Keep a suffix only when it materially disambiguates the player. */
12366:   const lastSuffix=tail.length>1&&suffix.test(tail[tail.length-1])?tail.pop():'';

12413: function hjBuildLineup(entries,season=HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON,week=hjCurrentWeek()){
12414:   const pool=entries.filter(entry=>Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)!==21).map(entry=>({entry,position:hjPlayerPosition(entry),score:hjEntrySeasonValue(entry,season,week)})).filter(item=>HJ_POSITION_ORDER[item.position]!=null).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score),used=new Set(),take=(position,count)=>{const found=pool.filter(item=>item.position===position&&!used.has(item.entry)).slice(0,count);found.forEach(item=>used.add(item.entry));return found},groups={};
12415:   groups.QB=take('QB',1);groups.RB=take('RB',2);groups.WR=take('WR',2);groups.TE=take('TE',1);groups.FLEX=pool.filter(item=>['RB','WR','TE'].includes(item.position)&&!used.has(item.entry)).slice(0,1);groups.FLEX.forEach(item=>used.add(item.entry));groups['D/ST']=take('D/ST',1);groups.K=take('K',1);
12416:   const values=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(groups).map(([key,items])=>[key,items.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.score,0)]));values.OVERALL=Object.values(values).reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0);return {groups,values};
12417: }
12418: function hjStrengthRows(data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){

12420:   keys.forEach(key=>{[...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.values[key]-a.values[key]||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((row,index,list)=>{row.ranks[key]=index&&Math.abs(row.values[key]-list[index-1].values[key])<.005?list[index-1].ranks[key]:index+1})});return rows;
12421: }
12422: function hjMatchupUnits(entries,week,season){
12423:  const keys=['QB','RB','WR','TE','FLEX','D/ST','K'],values=Object.fromEntries(keys.map(key=>[key,0])),players=[],missing=new Set();
12424:  for(const entry of entries.filter(e=>![20,21].includes(Number(e.lineupSlotId)))){const slot=Number(entry.lineupSlotId),position=hjPlayerPosition(entry),group=slot===0||slot===1?'QB':slot===2?'RB':slot===4?'WR':slot===6?'TE':[3,5,7,23,24].includes(slot)?'FLEX':slot===16?'D/ST':slot===17?'K':position,stat=hjMathCanonicalStat(entry,'week',week,season,1),value=stat?Number(stat.appliedTotal):null;if(value===null)missing.add(group);else if(values[group]!=null)values[group]+=value;players.push({entry,value})}
12425:  for(const key of missing)values[key]=null;
12426:  return {values,players,total:players.length&&!missing.size?Object.values(values).reduce((n,v)=>n+v,0):null};
12427: }

15989:         });
15990:         if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`ESPN visible-player projection request ${response.status}`);
15991:         const payload=await response.json();

15994:         });

16198: function hjRosterLiveSelectionState(selection,week,data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){
16199:   let total=0,started=false,allFinal=true,count=0;
16200:   (selection||[]).filter(Boolean).forEach(entry=>{
16201:     count++;
16202:     const state=hjPlayerGameState(hjPlayerTeam(entry)),actual=hjPlayerActualScore(entry,week,data),entryStarted=state==='in'||state==='post'||(Number.isFinite(actual)&&Math.abs(actual)>.0001);
16203:     if(entryStarted)started=true;
16204:     if(state!=='post')allFinal=false;
16205:     if(Number.isFinite(actual))total+=actual;
16206:   });
16207:   return {total,started,final:started&&count>0&&allFinal};
16208: }
16209: function hjRosterTeamLiveState(team,week,data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){

16215: }

16396: const HJ_STRENGTH_UNIT_WEIGHTS={QB:.16,RB:.24,WR:.24,TE:.16,FLEX:.12,'D/ST':.04,K:.04};
16397: function hjStrengthModelLabel(model){return model==='espn'?'ESPN':model==='vegas'?'Vegas':model==='actual'?'Actual':'Combo'}
16398: function hjStrengthModelNote(model){
16399:   if(model==='actual')return 'Actual uses each manager’s current ESPN starting lineup and ESPN season projections. Optimization gain shows the best ESPN lineup available on that roster.';
16400:   if(model==='espn')return 'ESPN optimizes each roster using ESPN season projections.';
16401:   if(model==='vegas')return 'Vegas optimizes using Vegas season projections, with ESPN used only when Vegas has no player number.';

16404: function hjStrengthSeasonScores(entry,season=HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON,week=hjCurrentWeek()){
16405:   const espn=hjEntrySeasonValue(entry,season,week),rawVegas=Number(season)===HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON?hjVegasProjection(entry,'season'):null,vegas=Number.isFinite(rawVegas)?rawVegas:espn,combo=Number.isFinite(rawVegas)&&Number.isFinite(espn)?(rawVegas+espn)/2:(Number.isFinite(espn)?espn:(Number.isFinite(rawVegas)?rawVegas:0));
16406:   return {espn:Number.isFinite(espn)?espn:0,vegas:Number.isFinite(vegas)?vegas:0,combo:Number.isFinite(combo)?combo:0,hasVegas:Number.isFinite(rawVegas)};
16407: }
16408: function hjStrengthEntryScore(entry,model,season,week){const scores=hjStrengthSeasonScores(entry,season,week);return model==='vegas'?scores.vegas:model==='combo'?scores.combo:scores.espn}
16409: function hjStrengthSlotUnit(slotId,entry){slotId=Number(slotId);if([0,1].includes(slotId))return 'QB';if(slotId===2)return 'RB';if(slotId===4)return 'WR';if(slotId===6)return 'TE';if([3,5,7,23,24].includes(slotId))return 'FLEX';if(slotId===16)return 'D/ST';if(slotId===17)return 'K';const pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry);return HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.includes(pos)?pos:'FLEX'}
16410: function hjStrengthOptimized(entries,model,season,week){
16411:   const scoreMode=model==='actual'?'espn':model,pool=entries.filter(entry=>Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)!==21).map(entry=>({entry,position:hjPlayerPosition(entry),score:hjStrengthEntryScore(entry,scoreMode,season,week)})).filter(item=>HJ_POSITION_ORDER[item.position]!=null).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score),used=new Set(),take=(position,count)=>{const found=pool.filter(item=>item.position===position&&!used.has(item.entry)).slice(0,count);found.forEach(item=>used.add(item.entry));return found},groups={};
16412:   groups.QB=take('QB',1);groups.RB=take('RB',2);groups.WR=take('WR',2);groups.TE=take('TE',1);groups.FLEX=pool.filter(item=>['RB','WR','TE'].includes(item.position)&&!used.has(item.entry)).slice(0,1);groups.FLEX.forEach(item=>used.add(item.entry));groups['D/ST']=take('D/ST',1);groups.K=take('K',1);
16413:   const selected=Object.values(groups).flat().map(item=>item.entry),values=Object.fromEntries(HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.map(key=>[key,(groups[key]||[]).reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.score,0)]));return {selected,groups,values,total:Object.values(values).reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)};
16414: }
16415: function hjStrengthActual(entries,season,week,model='espn',data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){
16416:   const plan=hjLineupPlan(data),selected=hjActualSelection(entries,plan).filter(Boolean),values=Object.fromEntries(HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.map(key=>[key,0]));
16417:   hjActualSelection(entries,plan).forEach((entry,index)=>{if(!entry)return;const unit=hjStrengthSlotUnit(plan[index]?.slotId,entry);if(values[unit]!=null)values[unit]+=hjStrengthEntryScore(entry,model,season,week)});return {selected,values,total:Object.values(values).reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)};
16418: }
16419: function hjStrengthHealth(selected){let penalty=0;selected.forEach(entry=>{const status=String(entry?.injuryStatus||hjPlayer(entry)?.injuryStatus||'').toUpperCase();if(!status||status==='NORMAL'||status==='ACTIVE')return;if(status.includes('IR')||status.includes('OUT')||status.includes('SUSP'))penalty+=22;else if(status.includes('DOUBT'))penalty+=14;else if(status.includes('QUEST'))penalty+=6;else penalty+=4});return Math.max(35,100-penalty)}
16420: function hjStrengthRankScore(rank,count){if(count<=1)return 100;return 50+50*((count-rank)/(count-1))}
16421: function hjStrengthBuildModel(data,model){
16422:   const week=hjCurrentWeek(data),rows=(data?.teams||[]).map(team=>{const manager=hjMatchManager(team,data)||hjOwnerName(team,data)||`Manager ${team.id}`,effective=hjEffectiveRoster(team,data),scoreModel=model==='actual'?'espn':model,optimal=hjStrengthOptimized(effective.entries,scoreModel,effective.season,week),actual=hjStrengthActual(effective.entries,effective.season,week,scoreModel,data),chosen=model==='actual'?actual:optimal,used=new Set(chosen.selected),bench=effective.entries.filter(entry=>Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)!==21&&!used.has(entry)).map(entry=>({entry,score:hjStrengthEntryScore(entry,scoreModel,effective.season,week)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score),benchTop=bench.slice(0,5),depthRaw=benchTop.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.score,0),drivers=effective.entries.filter(entry=>Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)!==21).map(entry=>({entry,score:hjStrengthEntryScore(entry,scoreModel,effective.season,week)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3),health=hjStrengthHealth(chosen.selected),bestPossible=optimal.total,gain=Math.max(0,bestPossible-actual.total);return {team,manager,...effective,model,scoreModel,values:chosen.values,starterRaw:chosen.total,depthRaw,benchTop,drivers,health,gain,actualTotal:actual.total,optimalTotal:optimal.total,unitRanks:{},components:{},power:0,powerRank:0}});
16423:   const count=rows.length||1;
16424:   [...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.starterRaw-a.starterRaw).forEach((row,index)=>row.starterRank=index+1);[...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.depthRaw-a.depthRaw).forEach((row,index)=>row.depthRank=index+1);
16425:   HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.forEach(key=>[...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.values[key]-a.values[key]||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((row,index)=>row.unitRanks[key]=index+1));
16426:   rows.forEach(row=>{const starter=hjStrengthRankScore(row.starterRank,count),depth=hjStrengthRankScore(row.depthRank,count),balance=Object.entries(HJ_STRENGTH_UNIT_WEIGHTS).reduce((sum,[key,weight])=>sum+hjStrengthRankScore(row.unitRanks[key]||count,count)*weight,0),health=row.health;row.components={starter,depth,balance,health};row.power=.55*starter+.20*depth+.15*balance+.10*health});
16427:   [...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.power-a.power||b.starterRaw-a.starterRaw).forEach((row,index)=>row.powerRank=index+1);return rows;
16428: }

16431: function hjStrengthUnitHTML(row,key,count){const rank=row.unitRanks[key]||count,fill=Math.max(12,Math.min(100,hjStrengthRankScore(rank,count))),best=rank===Math.min(...Object.values(row.unitRanks)),weak=rank===Math.max(...Object.values(row.unitRanks));return `<div class="hq-power-unit${best?' is-best':''}${weak?' is-weak':''}" title="${esc(key)} rank #${rank} of ${count}"><div class="hq-power-unit-head"><span>${esc(key)}</span><b>#${rank}</b></div><div class="hq-power-unit-meter"><i style="--unit-fill:${fill.toFixed(1)}%"></i></div></div>`}

16609: \n<script id="hj-projection-math-audit-script">
16610: /* ======================================================================
16611:    PROJECTION / ROSTER-STRENGTH MATH AUDIT
16612:    - Canonical ESPN season projections: projected + season split only.
16613:    - Never substitutes a weekly projection or appliedStatTotal for a season total.
16614:    - Fetches rostered-player season projections lazily and in small ID batches.
16615:    - Vegas scoring is adjusted to this league's live ESPN scoring settings when
16616:      those settings are available, while preserving source-only categories.
16617:    - Roster Strength continues to use the exact same lineup selection engine as
16618:      the Rosters tab; only the season-value math is normalized here.
16619:    ====================================================================== */
16620: const HJ_MATH_SEASON={key:'',byId:new Map(),loading:null,ready:false,error:'',updated:'',fetchedAt:0};
16621: const HJ_MATH_POS_ID={QB:'1',RB:'2',WR:'3',TE:'4',K:'5','D/ST':'16'};
16622: const HJ_MATH_STAT={passYards:3,passTds:4,interceptions:20,rushYards:24,rushTds:25,recYards:42,recTds:43,receptions:53,fumbles:72};
16623: const HJ_MATH_SOURCE_DEFAULT={passYards:.04,passTds:4,interceptions:-1,rushYards:.1,rushTds:6,recYards:.1,recTds:6,receptions:1,fumbles:-2};
16624: const HJ_MATH_FALLBACK={passYards:.04,passTds:4,interceptions:-2,rushYards:.1,rushTds:6,recYards:.1,recTds:6,receptions:1,fumbles:-2};

16626: function hjMathPlayer(entry){return entry?.player||entry?.playerPoolEntry?.player||(typeof hjPlayer==='function'?hjPlayer(entry):{})||{}}
16627: function hjMathStats(entry){const p=hjMathPlayer(entry);return Array.isArray(p?.stats)?p.stats:[]}
16628: function hjMathSeasonMatches(stat,season){return Number(stat?.seasonId??stat?.seasonValue)===Number(season)}
16629: function hjMathSource(stat){const raw=stat?.statSourceId??stat?.statTypeId;return Number(raw)}
16630: function hjMathSplit(stat){const raw=stat?.statSplitTypeId;return raw==null?null:Number(raw)}
16631: function hjMathCanonicalStat(entry,period='week',week=1,season=HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON,source=1){
16632:   const stats=hjMathStats(entry).filter(stat=>hjMathSource(stat)===Number(source)&&hjMathSeasonMatches(stat,season)&&hjNumber(stat?.appliedTotal)!==null);
16633:   if(period==='season'){
16634:     let exact=stats.find(stat=>hjMathSplit(stat)===0&&Number(stat?.scoringPeriodId)===0);
16635:     if(!exact)exact=stats.find(stat=>hjMathSplit(stat)==null&&Number(stat?.scoringPeriodId)===0);
16636:     if(exact)return exact;
16637:     return null;
16638:   }
16639:   const target=Number(week);
16640:   return stats.find(stat=>hjMathSplit(stat)===1&&Number(stat?.scoringPeriodId)===target)
16641:     ||stats.find(stat=>hjMathSplit(stat)==null&&Number(stat?.scoringPeriodId)===target)
16642:     ||null;
16643: }
16644: function hjMathEntryId(entry){const p=hjMathPlayer(entry);return String(p?.id||entry?.id||entry?.playerId||'')}
16645: function hjMathCachedEntry(entry){
16646:   const id=hjMathEntryId(entry),record=HJ_DATA.requests.get(`players:${HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON}:${hjCurrentWeek()}`);
16647:   if(record?.value){if(record.indexedValue!==record.value){record.byId=new Map(record.value.map(e=>[hjMathEntryId(e),e]));record.indexedValue=record.value;}if(record.byId.has(id))return record.byId.get(id);}
16648:   if(id&&HJ_MATH_SEASON.byId.has(id))return HJ_MATH_SEASON.byId.get(id);
16649:   if(id&&typeof HJ_PLAYER_DIRECTORY!=='undefined'&&HJ_PLAYER_DIRECTORY?.espnCache?.has(id)){const hit=HJ_PLAYER_DIRECTORY.espnCache.get(id);if(hit)return hit}
16650:   if(id&&typeof HJ_ESPN_HQ_POOL!=='undefined'&&HJ_ESPN_HQ_POOL?.byId?.has(id)){const hit=HJ_ESPN_HQ_POOL.byId.get(id);if(hit)return hit}
16651:   return null;
16652: }
16653: function hjMathEspnSeason(entry,season=HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON){
16654:   const cached=hjMathCachedEntry(entry),local=hjMathCanonicalStat(entry,'season',1,season,1),fromCache=cached?hjMathCanonicalStat(cached,'season',1,season,1):null;
16655:   const stat=fromCache||local;if(stat)return Number(stat.appliedTotal);
16700:   const sourceRates={...HJ_MATH_SOURCE_DEFAULT,receptions:row?.sourceScoring==='.5tep'?(position==='TE'?1.5:1):1};
16701:   const leagueRaw=hjMathVegasRaw(row,position,league),sourceRaw=hjMathVegasRaw(row,position,sourceRates),source=Number(row?.sourceProjection);
16702:   /* Source projection can include categories not printed in the stat columns
16703:      (for example projected two-point conversions). Preserve that residual, then
16704:      apply only the scoring-rule delta for the raw stats we can verify. */
16705:   return Number.isFinite(source)?source+(leagueRaw-sourceRaw):leagueRaw;
16706: };

16708: async function hjMathLoadRosterSeasonProjections(data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data,force=false){

16718:  }).catch(error=>{HJ_MATH_SEASON.error=String(error.message||error);return HJ_MATH_SEASON;}).finally(()=>{HJ_MATH_SEASON.loading=null;});

16783: /* Internal consistency audit. This never changes the UI; it records exactly
16784:    what was checked so a bad total can be diagnosed instead of guessed at. */
16785: window.hjProjectionMathAudit=function(data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){
16786:   const report={season:HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON,espnMissing:[],vegasMissing:[],teamChecks:[],errors:[]};if(!data)return report;const week=hjCurrentWeek(data);

16788:   ['actual','espn','vegas','combo'].forEach(model=>{try{const rows=hj3BuildModel(data,model);rows.forEach(row=>{const sum=Object.values(row.values||{}).reduce((a,b)=>a+(Number(b)||0),0),delta=Math.abs(sum-Number(row.starterRaw||0));report.teamChecks.push({model,manager:row.manager,total:row.starterRaw,unitSum:sum,delta,complete:row.selection?row.selection.length>0:false});if(delta>.01)report.errors.push(`${model} ${row.manager}: unit sum differs from starter total by ${delta.toFixed(2)}`)})}catch(error){report.errors.push(`${model}: ${error?.message||error}`)}});

16836: function hj4CandidateSeasonScore(player,model){
16837:   const m=hj4Model(model),e=Number(player?.espnSeason),v=Number(player?.vegasSeason),hasE=Number.isFinite(e),hasV=Number.isFinite(v);
16838:   if(m==='espn')return hasE?e:null;
16839:   if(m==='vegas')return hasV?v:(hasE?e:null);
16840:   return hasE&&hasV?(e+v)/2:(hasE?e:(hasV?v:null));

16842: function hj4CacheReady(data){

16845: function hj4ResetCache(data){
16846:   HJ4_CACHE.data=data;HJ4_CACHE.season=HJ_PROJECTION_STATE.season;HJ4_CACHE.projectionSeason=HJ_MATH_SEASON.fetchedAt;HJ4_CACHE.replVersion=HJ3_REPLACEMENT.version;HJ4_CACHE.models.clear();HJ4_CACHE.candidates=hj3CandidatePool(data);

16848: function hj4OptimalSeasonSelection(entries,plan,model,season,week){
16849:   const pool=entries.filter(e=>Number(e?.lineupSlotId)!==21&&HJ_POSITION_ORDER[hjPlayerPosition(e)]!=null),
16850:         order=plan.map((slot,index)=>({slot,index,eligible:HJ_OPT_SLOT_ELIGIBLE[Number(slot.slotId)]||[]})).sort((a,b)=>a.eligible.length-b.eligible.length||a.index-b.index),
16851:         memo=new Map();
16852:   function solve(i,mask){
16853:     if(i>=order.length)return {score:0,picks:[]};
16854:     const key=i+'|'+mask;if(memo.has(key))return memo.get(key);
16855:     const item=order[i];let best={score:-Infinity,picks:[]};
16856:     for(let pIndex=0;pIndex<pool.length&&pIndex<30;pIndex++){
16857:       if(mask&(1<<pIndex))continue;const entry=pool[pIndex],pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry);if(!item.eligible.includes(pos))continue;
16858:       const value=hj4EntryScore(entry,model,season,week);if(!Number.isFinite(value))continue;
16859:       const next=solve(i+1,mask|(1<<pIndex)),score=value+next.score;
16860:       if(score>best.score)best={score,picks:[{index:item.index,entry},...next.picks]};
16861:     }
16862:     /* Only leave a slot empty when there is no usable projected player for it. */
16863:     if(!Number.isFinite(best.score)){const next=solve(i+1,mask);best={score:next.score,picks:[{index:item.index,entry:null},...next.picks]}}
16864:     memo.set(key,best);return best;
16865:   }
16866:   const solved=solve(0,0),selected=Array(plan.length).fill(null);solved.picks.forEach(p=>selected[p.index]=p.entry);return selected;
16867: }
16868: function hj4SelectionTotals(selection,plan,model,season,week){
16869:   const values=Object.fromEntries(HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.map(k=>[k,0])),missing=[];let total=0;
16870:   selection.forEach((entry,index)=>{if(!entry)return;const score=hj4EntryScore(entry,model,season,week),unit=hjStrengthSlotUnit(plan[index]?.slotId,entry);if(!Number.isFinite(score)){missing.push(entry);return}if(values[unit]!=null)values[unit]+=score;total+=score});
16871:   return {values,total,missing,complete:missing.length===0};
16872: }
16873: function hj4ReplacementLevels(candidates,model){
16874:   const levels={};HJ3_SKILL_POS.forEach(pos=>{const vals=(candidates||[]).filter(p=>p.position===pos).map(p=>hj4CandidateSeasonScore(p,model)).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((a,b)=>b-a).slice(0,3);levels[pos]=hj3Median(vals)});return levels;
16875: }
16876: function hj4Depth(entries,selection,model,season,week,candidates){
16877:   const used=new Set(selection.filter(Boolean)),bench=entries.filter(e=>Number(e?.lineupSlotId)!==21&&!used.has(e)),levels=hj4ReplacementLevels(candidates,model),chosen=[],byPos={},taken=new Set();
16878:   const scored=bench.map(entry=>{const pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry),score=hj4EntryScore(entry,model,season,week),base=levels[pos],vorp=Number.isFinite(score)&&Number.isFinite(base)?Math.max(0,score-base):null;return {entry,pos,score,base,vorp}}).filter(x=>HJ3_SKILL_POS.includes(x.pos)&&Number.isFinite(x.vorp));
16879:   Object.entries(HJ4_BACKUP_LIMITS).forEach(([pos,limit])=>{const picks=scored.filter(x=>x.pos===pos).sort((a,b)=>b.vorp-a.vorp||b.score-a.score).slice(0,limit);byPos[pos]=picks.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.vorp,0);picks.forEach(x=>{chosen.push({...x,role:pos});taken.add(x.entry)})});
16880:   const flex=scored.filter(x=>['RB','WR','TE'].includes(x.pos)&&!taken.has(x.entry)).sort((a,b)=>b.vorp-a.vorp||b.score-a.score)[0]||null;
16881:   if(flex){chosen.push({...flex,role:'FLEX'});byPos.FLEX=flex.vorp}else byPos.FLEX=0;
16882:   const raw=chosen.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.vorp,0),ready=HJ3_SKILL_POS.every(pos=>Number.isFinite(levels[pos]));
16883:   return {raw,chosen,byPos,levels,ready};
16884: }
16885: function hj4BalanceScore(row,count){return Object.entries(HJ_STRENGTH_UNIT_WEIGHTS).reduce((sum,[key,w])=>sum+hjStrengthRankScore(row.unitRanks[key]||count,count)*w,0)}
16886: function hj4BuildModel(data,model){

16891:     return {team,manager,...effective,model,plan,selection,currentSelection:current,values:totals.values,starterRaw:totals.total,currentTotal:currentTotals.total,gap,depth,depthRaw:depth.raw,unitRanks:{},components:{},power:0,powerRank:0,teamValue:0};
16892:   });

16894:   [...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.starterRaw-a.starterRaw||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((r,i)=>r.starterRank=i+1);
16895:   [...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.depthRaw-a.depthRaw||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((r,i)=>r.depthRank=i+1);
16896:   HJ_STRENGTH_KEYS.forEach(key=>[...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.values[key]-a.values[key]||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((r,i)=>r.unitRanks[key]=i+1));
16897:   rows.forEach(r=>{r.balanceScore=hj4BalanceScore(r,count);r.teamValue=r.starterRaw+HJ4_DEPTH_DISCOUNT*r.depthRaw});
16898:   [...rows].sort((a,b)=>b.balanceScore-a.balanceScore||a.manager.localeCompare(b.manager)).forEach((r,i)=>r.balanceRank=i+1);
16899:   const leader=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.teamValue).filter(Number.isFinite),0);
16900:   rows.forEach(r=>{r.power=leader>0?100*r.teamValue/leader:0});

17030: function hj5ParseReplacementEntry(entry,week){
17031:   const p=hjEspnPoolEntryPlayer(entry),id=String(p?.id||entry?.id||''),position=hjEspnPoolPosition(entry),name=p?.fullName||p?.displayName||p?.name||'Player';
17032:   if(!HJ5_POSITIONS.includes(position)||!id)return null;

17036: async function hj5LoadReplacementPool(data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data,force=false){
17037:   if(!data)return [];
17038:   const key=`${HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON}:season`;
17039:   if(!force&&HJ5_REPLACEMENT.key===key&&HJ5_REPLACEMENT.loading)return HJ5_REPLACEMENT.loading;
17040:   if(!force&&HJ5_REPLACEMENT.key===key&&['ready','fallback'].includes(HJ5_REPLACEMENT.status))return HJ5_REPLACEMENT.players;
17041:   HJ5_REPLACEMENT.key=key;HJ5_REPLACEMENT.status='loading';HJ5_REPLACEMENT.error='';
17042:   const week=hjCurrentWeek(data),url=`https://lm-api-reads.fantasy.espn.com/apis/v3/games/ffl/seasons/${HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON}/segments/0/leagues/${ESPN_FANTASY_LEAGUE_ID}?scoringPeriodId=0&view=kona_player_info`,filter={players:{filterStatus:{value:['FREEAGENT','WAIVERS']},filterSlotIds:{value:[0,2,4,6,16,17]},filterStatsForSourceIds:{value:[1]},filterStatsForSplitTypeIds:{value:[0,1]},filterStatsForTopScoringPeriodIds:{value:18,additionalValue:[`00${HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON}`,`10${HJ_LEAGUE_SEASON}`]},sortPercOwned:{sortPriority:1,sortAsc:false},limit:500,offset:0}};
17043:   HJ5_REPLACEMENT.loading=(async()=>{
17044:     try{
17045:       const response=await fetch(url,{method:'GET',mode:'cors',credentials:'omit',cache:'no-store',headers:{'x-fantasy-filter':JSON.stringify(filter)}});
17046:       if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`ESPN replacement pool ${response.status}`);
17047:       const payload=await response.json(),rows=(Array.isArray(payload?.players)?payload.players:[]).map(e=>hj5ParseReplacementEntry(e,week)).filter(Boolean);
17048:       HJ5_REPLACEMENT.players=rows;HJ5_REPLACEMENT.status=rows.length?'ready':'fallback';if(!rows.length)HJ5_REPLACEMENT.error='ESPN returned no replacement players';
17049:     }catch(error){HJ5_REPLACEMENT.players=[];HJ5_REPLACEMENT.status='fallback';HJ5_REPLACEMENT.error=String(error?.message||error||'Replacement pool unavailable');console.warn('Roster Value replacement pool unavailable',error)}
17050:     HJ5_REPLACEMENT.version++;HJ5_REPLACEMENT.loading=null;return HJ5_REPLACEMENT.players;
17051:   })();
17052:   return HJ5_REPLACEMENT.loading;
17053: }
17054: function hj5CacheReady(data){return HJ5_CACHE.data===data&&HJ5_CACHE.season===HJ_PROJECTION_STATE.season&&HJ5_CACHE.projectionSeason===HJ_MATH_SEASON.fetchedAt&&HJ5_CACHE.replVersion===HJ5_REPLACEMENT.version}
17055: function hj5ResetCache(data){HJ5_CACHE.data=data;HJ5_CACHE.season=HJ_PROJECTION_STATE.season;HJ5_CACHE.projectionSeason=HJ_MATH_SEASON.fetchedAt;HJ5_CACHE.replVersion=HJ5_REPLACEMENT.version;HJ5_CACHE.models.clear();HJ5_CACHE.candidates=HJ5_REPLACEMENT.players.slice()}
17056: function hj5ReplacementLevels(model){
17057:   const levels={};HJ5_POSITIONS.forEach(pos=>{const vals=(HJ5_CACHE.candidates||[]).filter(p=>p.position===pos).map(p=>hj5CandidateScore(p,model)).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((a,b)=>b-a);levels[pos]=vals.length?vals[0]:null});return levels;
17058: }
17059: function hj5PlayerValue(entry,model,season,week,levels){
17060:   const pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry),projection=hj5EntryScore(entry,model,season,week),replacement=levels[pos];
17061:   if(!Number.isFinite(projection)||!Number.isFinite(replacement))return {projection,replacement,value:0,ready:false};
17062:   return {projection,replacement,value:Math.max(0,projection-replacement),ready:true};
17063: }
17064: function hj5OptimalSeasonSelection(entries,plan,model,season,week){
17065:   const pool=entries.filter(e=>Number(e?.lineupSlotId)!==21&&HJ_POSITION_ORDER[hjPlayerPosition(e)]!=null),order=plan.map((slot,index)=>({slot,index,eligible:HJ_OPT_SLOT_ELIGIBLE[Number(slot.slotId)]||[]})).sort((a,b)=>a.eligible.length-b.eligible.length||a.index-b.index),memo=new Map();
17066:   function solve(i,mask){
17067:     if(i>=order.length)return {score:0,picks:[]};const key=`${i}|${mask}`;if(memo.has(key))return memo.get(key);const item=order[i];let best={score:-Infinity,picks:[]};
17068:     for(let pIndex=0;pIndex<pool.length&&pIndex<30;pIndex++){
17069:       if(mask&(1<<pIndex))continue;const entry=pool[pIndex],pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry);if(!item.eligible.includes(pos))continue;const score=hj5EntryScore(entry,model,season,week);if(!Number.isFinite(score))continue;const next=solve(i+1,mask|(1<<pIndex)),total=score+next.score;if(total>best.score)best={score:total,picks:[{index:item.index,entry},...next.picks]};
17070:     }
17071:     if(!Number.isFinite(best.score)){const next=solve(i+1,mask);best={score:next.score,picks:[{index:item.index,entry:null},...next.picks]}}memo.set(key,best);return best;
17072:   }
17073:   const solved=solve(0,0),selected=Array(plan.length).fill(null);solved.picks.forEach(p=>selected[p.index]=p.entry);return selected;
17074: }

17084:     return {team,manager,...effective,model,plan,selection,currentSelection:current,playerValues,levels,positionValues,rosterValue,starterValue,benchValue,starterProjection,currentProjection,lineupGap:Math.max(0,starterProjection-currentProjection),flexEntry,flexValue,flexProjection,power:0,powerRank:0,starterRank:0,benchRank:0,positionRanks:{},flexRank:0};
17085:   });

17370: function hj7CompareHTML(data,rows){if(rows.length<2)return '<div class="hq-empty">Two rosters are required for comparison.</div>';const valid=id=>rows.some(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(id));if(!valid(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA))HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA=String(rows[0].team.id);if(!valid(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB)||HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB===HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA)HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB=String((rows[1]||rows[0]).team.id);const a=rows.find(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA))||rows[0],b=rows.find(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB))||rows[1],options=selected=>rows.map(r=>`<option value="${esc(r.team.id)}"${String(r.team.id)===String(selected)?' selected':''}>#${r.overallRank} ${esc(r.manager)}</option>`).join(''),diff=a.overall-b.overall,open=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareOpen;return `<div class="hj7-compare-controls"><select id="hq-strength-compare-a" aria-label="First manager">${options(a.team.id)}</select><div class="hj7-vs">VS</div><select id="hq-strength-compare-b" aria-label="Second manager">${options(b.team.id)}</select></div><article class="hj7-compare"><div class="hj7-compare-head"><div class="hj7-cteam">${av(a.manager)}<span><b>${esc(a.manager)}</b><small>#${a.overallRank} · ${pcFpts(a.overall)} pts</small></span></div><div class="hj7-cmid"><b>${Math.abs(diff)<.005?'EVEN':`${diff>0?'+':'−'}${pcFpts(Math.abs(diff))}`}</b><small>projected pts</small></div><div class="hj7-cteam right"><span><b>${esc(b.manager)}</b><small>#${b.overallRank} · ${pcFpts(b.overall)} pts</small></span>${av(b.manager)}</div></div><div class="hj7-crows">${['Overall',...HJ6_KEYS].map(k=>hj7CompareRow(a,b,k,open)).join('')}</div><button class="hj7-compare-toggle" type="button" data-hq-strength-compare-expand aria-expanded="${open}">${open?'Hide players':'Show players'}</button></article>`}
17371: function hj7Toolbar(data){const view=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.view==='compare'?'compare':'dashboard',model=['espn','vegas','combo'].includes(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.model)?HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.model:'combo',scope=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.scope==='all'?'all':'starters',horizon=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.horizon==='week'?'week':'season',week=hjCurrentWeek(data),group=(items,active,attr)=>`<div class="hj7-group">${items.map(([id,label,title])=>`<button type="button" class="hj7-toggle${active===id?' active':''}" ${attr}="${id}" aria-pressed="${active===id}"${title?` title="${esc(title)}"`:''}>${label}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;return `<div class="hj7-toolbar">${group([['dashboard','Dashboard'],['compare','Compare']],view,'data-hq-strength-view')}${group([['espn','ESPN'],['vegas','Vegas','Vegas uses ESPN fallback where Vegas has no projection.'],['combo','Combo','Combo averages ESPN and Vegas when both are available.']],model,'data-hq-strength-model')}${group([['all','All Players'],['starters','Starters']],scope,'data-hj6-scope')}${group([['season','Season'],['week',`Week ${week}`]],horizon,'data-hj6-horizon')}</div>`}

17445: <script id="hj-strength-v8-script">
17446: /* V8 changes only presentation/interaction. Ranking math remains the audited HJ6 Matrix engine. */
17447: function hj8RankFill(rank,count){const r=Math.max(1,Number(rank)||count||1),n=Math.max(1,Number(count)||1);if(typeof hjStrengthRankScore==='function')return Math.max(12,Math.min(100,hjStrengthRankScore(r,n)));return n<=1?100:Math.max(12,100-88*((r-1)/(n-1)))}
17448: function hj8PlayerRow(entry,row){const name=hj6PlayerName(entry),p=hjPlayer(entry)||{},id=p.id||entry?.playerId||'',team=hjPlayerTeam(entry),pos=hjPlayerPosition(entry),v=hj6Projection(entry,row.model,row.horizon,row.week,row.season),role=row.roleByEntry.get(entry)||'',selected=row.selected.has(entry),ir=Number(entry?.lineupSlotId)===21,attrs=ffnPlayerDataAttrs({id,name,team,position:pos,photo:hjPlayerPhoto(entry)});return `<button type="button" class="hj8-player pc-player-trigger${selected?' is-selected':''}" ${attrs}><span class="n">${esc(name)}${role==='FLEX'?'<span class="hj8-badge flex">FLEX</span>':''}${ir?'<span class="hj8-badge">IR</span>':''}</span><span class="v">${Number.isFinite(v)?pcFpts(v):'—'}</span></button>`}
17449: function hj8UnitEntries(row,key){return hj6UnitEntries(row,key)}
17450: function hj8UnitHTML(row,key,count,open=false){const rank=row.unitRanks[key]||count,total=Number(row.values[key])||0,entries=hj8UnitEntries(row,key),allRanks=Object.values(row.unitRanks||{}).filter(Number.isFinite),best=allRanks.length&&rank===Math.min(...allRanks),weak=allRanks.length&&rank===Math.max(...allRanks),fill=hj8RankFill(rank,count),list=open?`<div class="hj8-player-list">${entries.length?entries.map(e=>hj8PlayerRow(e,row)).join(''):'<div class="hj8-empty">None</div>'}</div>`:'';return `<section class="hj8-unit${best?' is-best':''}${weak?' is-weak':''}"><div class="hj8-unit-top"><span class="pos">${esc(key)}</span><span class="rk">#${rank}</span></div><div class="hj8-unit-points">${pcFpts(total)} pts</div><div class="hj8-meter"><i style="--fill:${fill.toFixed(1)}%"></i></div>${list}</section>`}
17451: function hj8DashboardHTML(data,rows){const count=rows.length||1;return `<div class="hj8-dashboard">${rows.map(row=>{const id=String(row.team.id),open=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.open.has(id),scopeCopy=row.scope==='starters'?'Projected starters':'All rostered players',horizonCopy=row.horizon==='season'?'season':'this week',missing=row.missing.length?` · ${row.missing.length} missing`:'';return `<article class="hj8-card${row.overallRank<=3?' is-top':''}${open?' is-open':''}" data-hj8-card="${esc(id)}"><div class="hj8-head"><div class="hj8-rank">${row.overallRank}</div><button class="hj8-person" type="button" data-hq-manager="${esc(id)}">${av(row.manager)}<span class="hj8-person-copy"><b>${esc(row.manager)}</b><small>${esc(scopeCopy)} · ${esc(horizonCopy)}${esc(missing)}</small></span></button><div class="hj8-overall"><strong>${pcFpts(row.overall)}</strong><small>Overall #${row.overallRank}</small></div><button class="hj8-expand" type="button" data-hj8-expand="${esc(id)}" aria-expanded="${open}" aria-label="${open?'Collapse':'Expand'} ${esc(row.manager)}">⌄</button></div><div class="hj8-rail-wrap"><div class="hj8-rail" data-hj8-rail>${HJ6_KEYS.map(k=>hj8UnitHTML(row,k,count,open)).join('')}</div></div></article>`}).join('')}</div>`}
17452: function hj8BarWidths(a,b){const max=Math.max(Math.abs(Number(a))||0,Math.abs(Number(b))||0,1);return {a:Math.max(2,50*(Math.abs(Number(a))||0)/max),b:Math.max(2,50*(Math.abs(Number(b))||0)/max)}}

17928: /* Broadcast-style UI on top of the audited HJ6 Matrix engine. */
17929: function hj12RankClass(rank,count){rank=Number(rank)||count||1;count=Number(count)||1;return rank===1?'rank-1':rank<=3?'rank-top':rank>=Math.max(2,count-1)?'rank-low':''}
17930: function hj12PositionEntries(row,key){if(key==='FLEX')return row.flexEntry?[row.flexEntry]:[];return hj6SortByProjection((row.entries||[]).filter(e=>hjPlayerPosition(e)===key),row.model,row.horizon,row.week,row.season)}

17939: function hj12CompareHTML(rows){if(rows.length<2)return '<div class="hq-empty">Two rosters are required for comparison.</div>';const valid=id=>rows.some(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(id));if(!valid(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA))HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA=String(rows[0].team.id);if(!valid(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB)||HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB===HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA)HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB=String((rows[1]||rows[0]).team.id);const a=rows.find(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareA))||rows[0],b=rows.find(r=>String(r.team.id)===String(HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareB))||rows[1],options=selected=>rows.map(r=>`<option value="${esc(r.team.id)}"${String(r.team.id)===String(selected)?' selected':''}>#${r.overallRank} ${esc(r.manager)}</option>`).join(''),diff=a.overall-b.overall,open=HJ_STRENGTH_STATE.compareOpen;return `<div class="hj12-compare-wrap"><div class="hj12-compare-controls"><select id="hq-strength-compare-a" aria-label="First manager">${options(a.team.id)}</select><div class="hj12-vs">VS</div><select id="hq-strength-compare-b" aria-label="Second manager">${options(b.team.id)}</select></div><article class="hj12-compare-card"><div class="hj12-compare-head"><div class="hj12-cteam">${av(a.manager)}<span><b>${esc(a.manager)}</b><small>#${a.overallRank} · ${pcFpts(a.overall)} pts</small></span></div><div class="hj12-cdelta"><b>${Math.abs(diff)<.005?'EVEN':`${diff>0?'+':'−'}${pcFpts(Math.abs(diff))}`}</b><small>projected pts</small></div><div class="hj12-cteam right"><span><b>${esc(b.manager)}</b><small>#${b.overallRank} · ${pcFpts(b.overall)} pts</small></span>${av(b.manager)}</div></div>${['Overall',...HJ6_KEYS].map(k=>hj12CompareRow(a,b,k,open)).join('')}<button class="hj12-compare-toggle" type="button" data-hj12-compare-expand aria-expanded="${open}">${open?'Hide players':'Show players'}</button></article></div>`}

19905: function hjActivityTransactionRows(payload,data=HJ_LEAGUE_STATE.data){
19906:   const rows=[];
19907:   for(const tx of (Array.isArray(payload?.transactions)?payload.transactions:[])){
19908:     if(String(tx?.status||'').toUpperCase()!=='EXECUTED')continue;
19909:     const txType=String(tx?.type||'').toUpperCase();
19910:     // Include every executed transaction with explicit ADD/DROP or trade items.
19911:     const at=Number(tx?.processDate||tx?.executionDate||tx?.proposedDate||tx?.date)||Date.now();
19912:     for(const item of (Array.isArray(tx?.items)?tx.items:[])){
19913:       const playerId=item?.playerId??item?.player?.id??item?.playerPoolEntry?.player?.id;
19914:       if(playerId==null)continue;
19915:       const itemType=String(item?.type||'').toUpperCase();
19916:       const fromId=Number(item?.fromTeamId)||0,toId=Number(item?.toTeamId)||0;
19917:       let type='',from='',to='';
19918:       if(txType==='TRADE'&&fromId&&toId&&fromId!==toId){type='Trade';from=hjActivityTeamName(fromId,data);to=hjActivityTeamName(toId,data)}
19919:       else if(itemType==='ADD'){type='Add';to=hjActivityTeamName(toId||tx?.teamId,data)}
19920:       else if(itemType==='DROP'){type='Drop';from=hjActivityTeamName(fromId||tx?.teamId,data)}
19921:       else continue;
19922:       const player=hjActivityPlayerById(playerId,data,item),sourceId=String(tx?.id||`${at}:${txType}`);
19923:       rows.push({id:`${sourceId}:${playerId}:${itemType}:${fromId}:${toId}`,transactionId:sourceId,at,type,player,from,to,source:txType});
19924:     }
19925:   }
19926:   return rows.sort((a,b)=>b.at-a.at);
19927: }

24063:  const rows=[];

24072:   }
